Title: Seconda speditione all'Indie Orientali di Monsignor Sebastiani Fr. Giuseppe di S. Maria dell'Ordine de' Carmelitani Scalzi, Vescovo di Hierapoli, Delegato Apostolico ne' Regni de' Malavari, ordinata da Alessandro VII di gloriosa memoria
Creator: Giuseppe di Santa Maria Sebastiani, O.C.D. (1623-1689), Bishop of Hierapolis, apostolic delegate and vicar apostolic to the St. Thomas Christians of Malabar 1661-1663 (first-person account)
Publisher: In Roma: Nella Stamperia di Filippo M. Mancini, con licenza de' Superiori
Publication Date: 1672 (the imprint line in the OCR reads 'Nella Stamperia di Filippo M. Mancini, 1671'; the approbation inside the volume is dated Rome, 6 March 1672; the host catalogue gives 1672)
Rights: Public Domain (published 1671 or 1672, more than 95 years before 2026; public domain by date)
Language: ita
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_XV38nWq7p0oC, file bub_gb_XV38nWq7p0oC_djvu.txt (Google scan of the National Central Library of Rome copy), fetched directly by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session), 2026-10-16; host rights statement: licenseurl http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/ (Public Domain Mark 1.0); no possible-copyright-status field.
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged apart from this header (no long-s characters present; the OCR renders the seventeenth-century long s as 'f' throughout and that pattern is left as found; nothing retyped, dropped or corrected). Rights basis restated in one checkable sentence: the imprint and approbation inside this file are dated 1671 and 6 March 1672, so the work is public domain by date whichever year is the true one.
Content note: Italian throughout, with some Latin documents (briefs and letters) printed in full. Three books, with an 'Indice de' capitoli' near the front: Libro Primo, the state of the Christianity of the Serra at the start of the second mission and the journey out by Malta, Aleppo, Nineveh, Baghdad, Basra, Persia, Surat and Goa to Cochin; Libro Secondo, the mission of 1661-1663 (the return of churches to obedience, the attempts against 'l'Intruso' Archdeacon Thomas, the Dutch sieges and the loss of Cranganore and Cochin, the author's dealings with the Dutch, the election and consecration of Alexander de Campo (Parambil Chandy) as Bishop of Megara, the sentence against the Archdeacon, and the author's departure); Libro Terzo, the return journey. A Carmelite bishop's eyewitness account written for Rome; primary for the mission side of 1661-1663 and for the consecration of 1663 that fixed the division of the community, hostile to the Archdeacon's party. OCR sampled at 10/30/50/70/90 percent and found readable but rough (share of word-like tokens 0.77); a second scan of the same edition exists (bub_gb_DSFEHdebzqMC, National Central Library of Florence; not sampled). Second witness for exact wording until a passage is checked against the page images. No public-domain English translation is known to exist (archive.org searched 2026-10-16: none found).
